# Wither Guard
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons, Mobs
[Wither Guard]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 285-609
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 50% (Normal only)
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
Mob Type: [[Skeletal]] (Normal Only) [[Wither]] Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]]) (MM Only)
Stats: [[Health]]: 3,500,000-300,000,000; [[Damage]]: 18,000-202,500
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Bone Dye]]
XP: 427-913 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 313-669
[Metadata]
ID: WITHER_GUARD_100; MASTER_WITHER_GUARD_100
The Wither Guard is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] boss fight.
## Spawning
Wither Guard spawns during [[Maxor]] and [[Storm]] boss phases in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
## Behavior
Wither Guard will try to shoot the player with a Bow from a large distance, dealing large amount of [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]]. Wither Guards cannot move.

The Wither Husk is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|The Master Mode Catacombs - Floor VII]].
## Spawning
Wither Husk spawns during [[The Wither King|Wither King]] boss phase in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|The Master Mode Catacombs - Floor VII]].
## Behavior
Wither Husks spawn sitting and invulnerable to player attacks. After boss fight start, they'll stand up and chase the player.

The Wither Miner is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] that spawns in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] boss fight.
## Spawning
Wither Miner spawns during [[Maxor]], [[Storm]] and [[The Wither King|Wither King]] boss phases in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]].
## Behavior
Wither Miner tries to hit the player in melee, dealing large amount of [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]]. Spawns a Wither Skull on death that flies to the player and deals high [[Damage]] if it hits. [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]

# Withermancer
Aliases: Crypt Witherlord
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Withermancer]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 131-573
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 80% (Except M4 ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]))
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Mob Type: [[Wither]] [[Skeletal]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 180,000-150,000,000; [[Damage]]: 1,879-216,000
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Bone Dye]] 0–1x [[Conjuring]]
XP: 196-859 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 144-630
[Metadata]
ID: CRYPT_WITHERSKELETON_#; MASTER_CRYPT_WITHERSKELETON_#
The Withermancer is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] rarely encountered in [[The Catacombs - Floor IV]] and above.
## Behavior
Withermancer takes 80% reduced [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]], making Magic Weapons less effective against it. It also has two orbiting skulls around it that track down players that get close to it, dealing damage if the skull connects with the player.

## Trivia
- The orbiting skulls of the Withermancer appear to reference the Bloons Tower Defense 5 Glaive lord's 4th tier upgrade on upgrade path 1 to have 2 orbiting glaives dealing immense damage to anything that touches them.
- If you kill the Withermancer before it's skull hits, the skull will despawn. Additionally, skulls despawn after 10 seconds of pursuing a player.
- When it is defeated, it will drop a skull stack like any other dungeon skeleton.

# Zombie Commander
Aliases: Commander
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons, Dungeon Mobs
[Zombie Commander]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 221-613
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,700,000-250,000,000; [[Damage]]: 5,632-256,000
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Premium Flesh]] 0–1x [[Zombie Commander Whip]] 0–1x Zombie Commander Armor Piece ([[Zombie Commander Armor]])
XP: 331-919 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 243-674
[Metadata]
ID: ZOMBIE_COMMANDER_#; MASTER_ZOMBIE_COMMANDER_#
Zombie Commanders are a stronger variant of [[Zombie Knight|Zombie Knights]]. They attack the player with their whip, capable of doing ranged and melee damage. They drop [[Zombie Commander Armor]] and the [[Zombie Commander Whip]].
These mobs spawn on the [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]] and [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]]. They don't spawn often on Floor VI, while they appear regularly on Floor VII. When a Zombie Commander is attacking, they strafe from left to right, making them harder to hit. They also are able to soak up multiple hits with their high health. However, since their damage is relatively minimal, they do not pose a threat if you have the proper gear.

## Trivia
- Their form of attack/AI is reused in the [[Phantom Fisher]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]], with the Zombie Commander having significantly higher damage however, which is understandable, as it is a Dungeon Mob.
- It's drops are ironically less valuable than its weaker variants, the [[Zombie Knight Armor]] and [[Zombie Soldier Armor]], as the armor has a higher [[Catacombs]] requirement, as well as lacking [[Damage]] bonuses. However, it may serve as a decent budget armor for [[Effective Health]].

# Zombie Grunt
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs, Dungeons, Dungeon Mobs
[Zombie Grunt]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 40-286
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 10%
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Entrance]]-Floor II ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]])
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 7,000-3,000,000; [[Damage]]: 201-21,600
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x Rotten Armor Piece ([[Rotten Armor]]) 0–1x [[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]] 0–1x [[Premium Flesh]]
XP: 60-429 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 44-314
[Metadata]
ID: ZOMBIE_GRUNT_#; MASTER_ZOMBIE_GRUNT_#
The Zombie Grunt is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] commonly found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] [[The Catacombs - Entrance|Entrance]], [[The Catacombs - Floor I|Floor I]] and [[The Catacombs - Floor II|Floor II]]. They notably drop [[Rotten Armor]], a decent early-game armor set.
Like other [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], their [[Health|HP]] scales with the floor they spawn in. They can also have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified. Healthy increases the mob's [[Health|HP]]. Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]]. Flaming causes attacks to set the player on fire. Speedy increases movement speed. Stormy causes lightning to strike nearby players, dealing [[True Damage]].

XP: 156-715 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 114-524
[Metadata]
ID: ZOMBIE_KNIGHT_#; MASTER_ZOMBIE_KNIGHT_#
The Zombie Knight is a [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mob]] found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] that activates a [[Enchantments|Lifesteal-like]] effect when attacking a player. They can drop [[Zombie Knight Armor]] or a [[Zombie Knight Sword]] upon death.
Its health, like any other [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], scale on which floor they spawn in. It can also have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified. Healthy increases the mob's [[Health]]. Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]]. Flaming makes melee attacks light the player on fire. Speedy makes the mob move much faster. Stormy makes the mob strike lightning on nearby players, dealing true damage.

The Zombie Lord is a mob found only in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]. It drops [[Zombie Lord Armor]].
It can have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified. Healthy increases the mob's [[Health]]. Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]]. Flaming makes melee attacks light the player on fire. Speedy makes the mob move much faster. Stormy makes the mob strike lightning on nearby players, dealing [[True Damage]].

## Trivia
- This mob has the highest [[Health]] of any normal dungeon mob, and may take multiple hits even with powerful gear to slay.
- It is classified as an undead, as well as many other dungeon mobs, meaning weapons with [[Smite]] will do additional damage to it, making it worth considering to add the [[Smite]] [[Enchantments|enchantment]] to your weapon.
  - It also affected by the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Zombie Pet]] Living Dead Ability, which gives 20% more damage to Zombies, however it is not recommended as most of the Zombie Pet's perks are pointless inside [[Dungeons]].

# Zombie Soldier
Categories: Dungeon Enemies, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Mobs, Catacombs
[Zombie Soldier]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 94-463
Damage Resistance: Magic Damage ([[Damage Calculation]]): 10%
Spawn Condition: [[The Catacombs - Floor III]]-VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 77,000-36,000,000; [[Damage]]: 738-111,600
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x Zombie Soldier Armor Piece ([[Zombie Soldier Armor]]) 0–1x [[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]] 0–1x [[Zombie Soldier Cutlass]] 0–1x [[Premium Flesh]]
XP: 141-694 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 103-509
[Metadata]
ID: ZOMBIE_SOLDIER_#; MASTER_ZOMBIE_SOLDIER_#
Zombie Soldier is a stronger version of a [[Zombie Grunt]]. It is faster and stronger than it. It spawns on [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] and above. It drops the [[Zombie Soldier Armor]] and the [[Zombie Soldier Cutlass]].
Its health, like any other [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], scale on which floor they spawn in. It can also have modifiers such as Healthy, Flaming, Stormy, Speedy or Fortified. Healthy increases the mob's [[Health]]. Fortified increases the mob's [[Defense]]. Flaming makes melee attacks light the player on fire. Speedy makes the mob move much faster. Stormy makes the mob strike lightning on nearby players, dealing [[True Damage]].

# The Catacombs - Entrance
Aliases: Catacombs Entrance (Dungeon Floor), Entrance, F0, FE, The Catacombs - Entrance (Dungeon Floor), The Catacombs Entrance (Dungeon Floor)
Categories: Dungeon Floors, Catacombs, Dungeons
[The Catacombs - Entrance]
Type: Dungeon Floor
Dungeon: Catacombs
Boss: [[The Watcher]]
Requirements to enter: Combat XV (15) ([[Combat]])
Base XP: 50
The Catacombs - Entrance is the starting [[Dungeons]] floor. There is no final boss, so the dungeon ends when [[The Watcher]] is defeated.
## Mechanics
The player can enter The Catacombs - Entrance once they have [[Combat|Combat XV (15)]], and must enter with a party of 2-5 players. Once the player has defeated [[The Professor]] on [[The Catacombs - Floor III|Floor III]] and given [[Mort]] a [[Saul's Recommendation]], they may queue a solo dungeon.
### Boss
Since there is no boss at the end of the dungeon, once [[The Watcher]] is killed, the dungeon ends. This also means there is no post-boss dungeon chest to open. Additionally, class milestones are disabled in The Catacombs - Entrance.
### Death
Players are automatically revived after 50s, unless another player uses a [[Revive Stone]] on them or the ghost punches a fairy until it dies.
## Map
The Catacombs - Entrance is a tiny dungeon, and the map is of a 4x4.
### Puzzles
The following puzzles have a chance to appear in The Catacombs - Entrance:
|Puzzle|
|-|
|Creeper Beams|
|Teleport Maze|
|Three Weirdos|
|Water Board|
|Tic Tac Toe|
## Entities
Mobs fill the dungeon floor, but [[NPC|NPCs]] apart from [[Mort]] also have a chance to appear in the dungeon.
### NPCs
[[Mort]] will spawn in front of the door to the dungeon, allowing the party to ready-up, and will offer players a [[Dungeon Orb]] who do not already have one in their inventory. The following [[NPC|NPCs]] have a chance to spawn in a room.
- [[Duncan]]
- [[Tomioka]]
- [[Trinity]]
- [[Winona]]
### Mobs
[[Mob|Mobs]] fill most dungeon rooms. They can have a modifier which affects their behavior and loot. The following mobs have a chance to spawn in a room.
- [[Angry Archaeologist]]: 9,500 [[Health]]
- [[Bat (disambiguation)|Bat]]: 100 [[Health]]
- [[Cellar Spider]]: 22,000 [[Health]]
- [[Crypt Dreadlord]]: 19,000 [[Health]]
- [[Crypt Lurker]]: 12,000 [[Health]]
- [[Crypt Undead]]: 45,000 [[Health]]
- [[Lonely Spider]]: 5,000 [[Health]]
- [[Lost Adventurer]]: 60,000-175,000 [[Health]]
- [[Scared Skeleton]]: 19,000 [[Health]]
- [[Skeleton Grunt]]: 18,000 [[Health]]
- [[Sniper]]: 13,000 [[Health]]
- [[Undead Skeleton]]: 25,000 [[Health]]
- [[Zombie Grunt]]: 22,000 [[Health]]

# Blood Key
Categories: Dungeons, Catacombs
[Blood Key]
A Blood Key is an item that can be obtained in the [[Catacombs]].
A Blood Key can used to open a [[The Watcher|Blood Door]] in the [[Catacombs]], which leads to the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] where [[The Watcher]] can be fought. The Blood Key is dropped by either a [[Minibosses|miniboss]], such as the [[Lost Adventurer]] or [[Angry Archaeologist]], or when all [[Mob|Mobs]] with a star have been slain.
Clicking a [[The Watcher|Blood Door]] with the Blood Key consumes the Blood Key and opens the door. The Blood Key is usable by any person within the dungeon, regardless of whether or not they are the player who obtained it.
## Trivia
- The Blood Key and [[Overflux Power Orb]] use the same texture.
## History

# Classes
Aliases: Class
Categories: Dungeons, Classes, Catacombs, Abilities
Classes are roles a player can have in [[Dungeons]], each with their own effects and bonuses. The available classes are [[Tank]], [[Berserk]], [[Mage]], [[Healer]], and [[Archer]]. The teams of people must be at least 4-5 people for all floors. There can be multiple of the same class in the same dungeon at once. If there is only one person who chose a role (for example T, B, M, H, A as layout), those people get doubled strength of their class's abilities.
## Bonuses
- Class Passives - As the name implies, these effects are granted automatically.
- Dungeon Stone Abilities - Abilities that are activated using the Dungeon Orb. Right clicking or ctrl-dropping activates your normal ability, while left clicking or dropping activates your ultimate ability.
- Ghost Abilities - Abilities that you can use when you die.
## Classes
### Healer
The Healer Dungeon Orb
[[Healer]] is a dungeon class that specializes in healing and support.
### Mage
The Mage Dungeon Orb
[[Mage]] is a dungeon class that specializes in dealing high Magic and area of effect damage. Most Magic Weapons have strong area of effect abilities that can clear out huge waves of [[Mob|mobs]] but in return struggle with tankier mobs such as mini bosses.
### Berserk
The Berserk Dungeon Orb
[[Berserk]] is a dungeon class that specializes in dealing melee damage to single targets. While they do not deal the highest damage in mid game, their late game single target damage per seconds should not be underestimated and is said to be the class with the highest damage per seconds in dungeons as well as their ability to heal from attacking allows them to fight and survive against the toughest of enemies.
### Archer
The Archer Dungeon Orb
[[Archer]] is a Dungeon class that specializes in dealing extreme [[Damage]] through ranged attacks using [[Bows]]. It shares the area-clearing ability of the Mage and the tank-slaying ability of the Berserker, but, in return, is extremely fragile. This class relies heavily on [[Crit Damage]] and [[Strength]], much like the Berserk class. It is the best class for damage per second.
### Tank
The Tank Dungeon Orb
[[Tank]] is a dungeon class that specializes in [[Effective Health]] and being able to take large amount of damage and keeping mobs off their teammates.
## History

# Dungeon Blessing
Aliases: Blessing, Blessing of Time, Blessing of time, Blessings, Dungeon Blessings
Categories: Dungeons, Catacombs
[Dungeon Blessing]
Dungeon Blessings are stat boosts found inside Dungeons that boost certain stats of all team members for the remainder of the run.
# Obtaining
Blessings can be obtained from [[Dungeons]] through multiple methods:
- Killing the final ✯ Starred mob in a [[Catacombs Rooms|room]], possibly a [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]]
- Killing a [[Bat (disambiguation)|Bat]]
- Opening a [[Dungeon Secrets|Dungeon Secret Chest]] or a [[Catacombs Puzzle Rooms|Dungeon Puzzle Chest]]
- Completing the [[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms|Quiz]] puzzle, on [[The Catacombs - Floor IV|Floor Ⅳ]] and above in both "NORMAL" and [[Catacombs|MASTER]] modes
- Returning the [[Wizard's Crystal]] to the [[Wizard (Catacombs)|Wizard]]
# Mechanics
Stats from blessings are always granted as a percentage boost to the player's current [[Stats]]. If a player's affected base stat changes by swapping [[Armor]] or [[Weapons|Weapon]], or otherwise, the additional stats from the Blessing will accordingly scale.
The strength of a blessing depends on its tier and the floor it was found in. [[Catacombs Mobs|Mobs]] and [[Catacombs Puzzle Rooms|Puzzles]] always grant Level Ⅴ (5) blessings, while [[Dungeon Secrets|Secrets]] always grant level Ⅰ (1) or Ⅱ (2) blessings.
Blessings increase in strength with increasing floor number. However, blessings in [[Catacombs|MASTER MODE]] do not scale up from [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] blessings, and instead match their corresponding "NORMAL" MODE floor.
## Types
There are 5 main types of Dungeon Blessing that can be found inside Dungeons:
|Name|Stats given|Percent incr.|Flat incr.|Obtaining||
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Blessing of Life|[[Health]]|Level × 3%||Killing the final ✯ Starred mob in a room Opening a Dungeon Secret Chest ([[Dungeon Secrets]]) or a Dungeon Puzzle Chest ([[Catacombs Puzzle Rooms]]) Killing a Bat ([[Bat (disambiguation)]])||
|Blessing of Life|[[Health Regen]]|Level × 3%||||
|Blessing of Power|[[Strength]]|Level × 2%|Level × 4|(same as above)||
|Blessing of Power|[[Crit Damage]]|Level × 2%|Level × 4|(same as above)||
|Blessing of Stone|[[Defense]]|Level × 2%|Level × 4|(same as above)||
|Blessing of Stone|[[Damage]]||Level × 6|(same as above)||
|Blessing of Wisdom|[[Intelligence]]|Level × 2%|Level × 4|(same as above)|Killing the final ✯ Starred mob in a room Opening a Dungeon Secret Chest ([[Dungeon Secrets]]) or a Dungeon Puzzle Chest ([[Catacombs Puzzle Rooms]]) Killing a Bat ([[Bat (disambiguation)]]) Returning the [[Wizard's Crystal]] to the Wizard ([[Wizard (Catacombs)]])|
|Blessing of Wisdom|[[Speed]]||Level × 4|||
|Blessing of Time|[[Health]]|Level × 2%|Level × 4|Completing the Quiz ([[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms]]) puzzle|(same as above)|
|Blessing of Time|[[Defense]]|Level × 2%|Level × 4|Completing the Quiz ([[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms]]) puzzle||
|Blessing of Time|[[Strength]]|Level × 2%|Level × 4|Completing the Quiz ([[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms]]) puzzle||
|Blessing of Time|[[Intelligence]]|Level × 2%|Level × 4|Completing the Quiz ([[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms]]) puzzle||
## Stacking
The effect of the same blessing stacks with each other. For example, if a team finds a Blessing of Life Ⅱ (2) and later obtains a Blessing of Life Level Ⅴ (5), they keep the effect of both blessings, and the active blessing will display in the widgets as a Blessing of Life Level Ⅶ (7).
## Calculation
The effect of a [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessing]] is increased by its level and the following:
|Origin|Increase|Text|
|-|-|-|
|Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]) [[Paul]]'s Benediction perk|25%|Blessings are x% stronger.|
|Wither Essence Shop ([[Wither Essence]]) perk [[Dungeon Blessing]] Forbidden Blessing|1%→10%|Blessings are x% more effective on the player.|
|Dungeon Floor ([[Dungeons]]) buff|20%|Blessings are x% more effective when on Floor Ⅲ ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]]) or higher.|
|Faker ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Mimic Shard]])|1%→10%|Blessings are x% stronger on the player.|
|Max Increase|81.5%||
The buffs all multiply eachother, meaning the product of the increases is applied on the flat stat increase and percentage stat increase. The sum of all flat buffs is applied first, after which the product of all percentage stat increases is applied.
Example 1: Blessing of Power
Scenario:
- The Blessing of Power level is IX (9)
- Paul is not mayor
- Forbidden Blessing level IV (4) is active
- The Player is in The Catacombs Floor VI
- The Player has 1000 [[Strength]] before Blessings
Calculation:
1. Calculating the Blessing Effect Increase Blessing Effect Increase=(1+Forbidden Blessing Buff)×(1+Benediction Buff)×(1+Floor Buff)=(1+4%)×(1)×(1+20%)=1.248
2. Calculating the Blessing Flat Stat Increase Blessing Flat Increase=Blessing Level×Blessing Flat AmountLevel×Blessing Effect Increase=9×4×1.248=44.928
3. Calculating the Blessing Percent Stat Increase Blessing Percent Increase=Blessing Level×Blessing Percent AmountLevel×Blessing Effect Increase=9×2%×1.248=22.464%
4. Applying the Blessing Flat Increase Strengthpost-flat-buff=Strengthpre-flat-buff+Blessing Flat Increase=1000+44.928=1044.928
5. Applying the Blessing Percent Increase Strengthpost-percent-buff=Strengthpost-flat-buff×(1+Blessing Percent Increase)=1044.928×(1+22.464%)≈1279.66
Example 2: Blessing of Power and Blessing of Time
Scenario:
- The Blessing of Power level is XXIX (29)
- The Blessing of Time level is V (5)
- Paul is mayor with the Benediction perk active
- Forbidden Blessing level X (10) is active
- The Player is in The Master Catacombs Floor VII
- The Player has 6000 [[Strength]] before Blessings
Calculation:
1. Calculating the Blessing Effect Increase Blessing Effect Increase=(1+Forbidden Blessing Buff)×(1+Benediction Buff)×(1+Floor Buff)=(1+10%)×(1+25%)×(1+20%)=1.65
2. Calculating the Blessing Flat Stat Increase Blessing Flat Increase=∑Blessings(Blessing Level×Blessing Flat AmountLevel×Blessing Effect Increase)=29×4×1.65+5×4×1.65=191.4+33=224.4
3. Calculating the Blessing Percent Stat Increase Blessing Percent Increase=∏Blessings(1+Blessing Level×Blessing Percent AmountLevel×Blessing Effect Increase)−1=(1+29×2%×1.65)×(1+5×2%×1.65)−1=1.957×1.165−1=2.279905−1=127.9905%
4. Applying the Blessing Flat Increase Strengthpost-flat-buff=Strengthpre-flat-buff+Blessing Flat Increase=6000+224.4=6224.4
5. Applying the Blessing Percent Increase Strengthpost-percent-buff=Strengthpost-flat-buff×(1+Blessing Percent Increase)=6224.4×(1+127.9905%)≈14191.04

# Dungeon Puzzle Rooms
Aliases: Three Weirdos
Categories: Dungeons, Catacombs
An example of a puzzle room: the Three Weirdos puzzle.
Dungeon Puzzle Rooms contain Dungeon puzzles to be solved.
## Mechanics
2 or more puzzle rooms will generate in each dungeon. They are marked as the purple rooms on the [[Magical Map]]. Two of the same puzzle room will never generate in the same dungeon.
Each puzzle room contains a simple puzzle for players to solve. Completing a puzzle rewards the team with a Tier V [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessing]], as well as marking the puzzle on the Magical Map as complete. Failing to complete a puzzle subtracts 14 points from the team's Skill score.
The status of puzzles in the current Dungeon can be found in the tab menu. There, players can see the total number of puzzles in the Dungeon, their names, their states (incomplete, complete, or failed), and if failed, who was responsible.
## Puzzle Rooms
This includes all puzzles as well as what dungeon they correspond to.
### The Catacombs
|Name|Floor|Description|Failable|Secrets|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Tic Tac Toe|All|Players must tie a match of Tic Tac Toe against a computer. The computer always starts first. On Floors below 3, it will only begin in the center. On Floor 3 and above, it can start in the corner too.|✔ Yes|1|
|Three Weirdos|All|Three NPCs stand in a room with three corresponding chests. They will make statements about who has the correct chest, but one or more are lying. Players must use the given information to open the correct chest.|✔ Yes|0|
|Creeper Beams|All|Sea lanterns are placed on the walls and ceiling of the room. They can be joined with a beam by shooting them. Players must get four different beams to pass through the creeper in the middle to complete the room.|✕ No|0|
|Water Board|All|Three of the five doors to the chest are closed. By making water flow into the hole with the corresponding color on the water maze, players can toggle the doors open and closed. Six levers retract and extend their corresponding blocks on the water maze, and another lever located at the back of the room toggles the flow of water on and off. Players must use the levers to control the flow of water to enter the correct holes and have all five doors open at the same time.|✕ No|0|
|Teleport Maze|All|There are seven rooms with four teleport pads each, all leading to different rooms. Players must use the teleport pads to reach the chest in the center. When players teleport, they will face the teleport pad which leads to the center.|✕ No|0|
|Higher or Lower|2+|Ten Blazes will spawn in a column each with a different amount of HP. Players must kill the blazes in order, depending on the chest position in the room: lowest to highest HP if chest is on the bottom, or highest to lowest HP if chest is on the top. Killing a blaze out of order causes the puzzle to be failed.|✔ Yes|1|
|Boulder|3+|Pushing a button on each of the boxes will push it in that direction. Players must clear a path to the chest at the back of the room by pushing boxes correctly. It is possible to move the boxes in such a way that it becomes impossible to complete the puzzle.|✔ Yes|0|
|Ice Path|3+|The player must guide a silverfish across a slippery board to a gate by hitting it. The silverfish will go as far as possible in a straight line in the direction the player is facing, until it hits a block. Players can fail this puzzle if they place traps in attempt to cheese the puzzle. (This is not to be confused with the "Ice Fill" puzzle, the latter containing three stages of ice.)|✔ Yes|0|
|Quiz|4+|A statue called Oruo the Omniscent will ask three questions regarding SkyBlock Trivia, such as Fairy Souls, NPC Names and statuses of Catacombs bosses. If the player gets all of them right, the team will get a Blessing of Time, granting [[Health]], [[Defense]], [[Intelligence]], and [[Strength]]. This has the added effect of maxing out all the players' Training Weights in their inventories, as well as incrementing the timer for evolvable items by 1 hour (or 2 if the item is in the [[Time Pocket]]).|✔ Yes|0|
|Ice Fill|7|The player must turn all the ice into packed ice by walking over it for three stages. The player may step on only one block at a time and cannot move diagonally. It is recommended to move slowly as, even in a straight line, the game will sometimes think the player tried to move diagonally if the player goes too fast. (This is not to be confused with the "Ice Path" puzzle, the latter containing a silverfish.)|✕ No|0|
|Bomb Defuse|Removed|A 2-player puzzle. Players must first activate both pressure plates, which starts a 2-minute timer. The player on the right has the solutions to the locks, and they must communicate those answers to the player on the left, who must input those answers to unlock the doors. Once finished, both players must stand on the pressure plates at the back, which grants them access to the 2 blessings. If one does not finish this puzzle under 2 minutes, the puzzle will be considered failed.|✔ Yes|0|

# Star Upgrades
Aliases: Dungeonize, Essence Crafting, Star Upgrade, Stars
Categories: Dungeons, Catacombs, Mechanics
Star Upgrades are upgrades that can be applied to certain items to give them increased boosts to their stats.
## Mechanics
Stars are applied to items through the Essence Crafting at a blacksmith ( [[Malik]], [[Igor]], [[Alwin]], or [[The Hex]]). They require certain [[Essence]], items, and/or [[Coins]] to be added to items. The amount of resources required increases as more Stars are added. Items that can be upgraded with Stars are listed in the [[Essence Cost|Essence Guide]].
|Star|Condition|
|-|-|
|✪|Default|
|➊-➎|First through Fifth Master Stars ([[Master Star]])|
|✪|6th-10th Star on an item|
|✪|11th-15th Star on an item Currently only possible with Crimson Essence ([[Essence]]) Unique stars only possible with infernal [[Kuudra]] armor|
### Dungeon Items
#### Normal Stars
Dungeon items that have Stars applied to them gain +10% of their stats per Star while in [[Dungeons]]. The Stats of a Starred item while in Dungeons are displayed in dark gray parentheses after the base stats of the item on its tooltip. Up to 5 Stars can be applied to Dungeon Items.
#### Master Stars
Up to 5 [[Master Star|Master Stars]] can be applied to Dungeon items in an [[Anvil]], to give them +5% of their stats per Star while in Master Mode Dungeons.
### Outside of Dungeons
Items with Stars also gain +2% of their Stats per Star regardless if they are dungeonised or not. Typically, non-dungeon items may require additional items such as [[Leather Cloth]] or [[Wither Soul]] to be upgraded, and most [[Crimson Isle]] items requires [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]] to upgrade past 7 stars.
## Trivia
- Most items which can have Stars applied to them can have up to 5/10/15 applied depending on item, with the exception of [[Dojo|Dojo Belts]] which can have up to 2/4/6/8/10/12 applied based on the tier of the belt, and [[Magma Rod|Magma]]/[[Inferno Rod|Inferno]]/[[Hellfire Rod|Hellfire]] Rods, which can have 3/7/10 respectively.
- Each tier of [[Kuudra]] Armor must have 10 stars applied before it can be prestiged to the next tier, clearing all previous stars. Upon reaching infernal tier, 15 final stars can be applied.
- Master Stars do not provide any bonuses outside dungeons.

# 2x Powder
Categories: Dwarven Mines
2x Powder is a passive event that can happen in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Mechanics
During the 2x Powder event, all ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]], ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] and ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] drops are doubled. This includes ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] gained from [[Fetchur]], the [[Puzzler]], [[Commissions]] and the Daily Powder [[Heart of the Mountain]] perk, even though the message still states the original amount of powder.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Event announced [[Crystal Hollows]]|⚑ The 2x Powder event starts in 20 seconds! This is a passive event! It's happening everywhere in the Crystal Hollows!|
|Event announced [[Dwarven Mines]]|⚑ The 2x Powder event starts in 20 seconds! This is a passive event! It's happening everywhere in the Dwarven Mines!|
|Event starting|----------------------------------------------------- 2X POWDER STARTED! Passive Active Event Everybody gets twice as much powder of all types! -----------------------------------------------------|
|Event ending|----------------------------------------------------- 2X POWDER ENDED! Passive Active Event Powder amount is back to normal, but there will be another event soon! -----------------------------------------------------|
## Commission
The [[Commissions|2x Mithril Powder Collection]] [[Commissions|Commission]] requires the player to collect 500 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]], during the 2x Powder event. If the player receives the commission after collecting 500 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]], the Commission will be immediately completed, as long as the event is still running.
## Gallery
- 2x Powder Commission
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added 2x Powder Event.|

# Better Together
Categories: Dwarven Mines, Events
The Better Together is a passive event that can happen in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Mechanics
The Better Together event lasts for 20 minutes. For every player in the same zone, players gain 250 [[Mining Speed]] and 20 [[Mining Fortune]]. The buff maxes out at 5 players.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Event announced [[Crystal Hollows]]|⚑ The Better Together event starts in 20 seconds! This is a passive event! It's happening everywhere in the Crystal Hollows!|
|Event announced [[Dwarven Mines]]|⚑ The Better Together event starts in 20 seconds! This is a passive event! It's happening everywhere in the Dwarven Mines!|
|Event starting|----------------------------------------------------- BETTER TOGETHER STARTED! Passive Active Event The Better Together Festival is underway! While this event is active, gain +250 ⸕ Mining Speed and +20 ☘ Mining Fortune for each player in the same zone as you! (Max 5 players) ----------------------------------------------------- You can hear cooperative chattering coming from town… The Better Together festival is active for 20 minutes!|
|Event ending|----------------------------------------------------- BETTER TOGETHER ENDED! Passive Active Event The festival has ended, but the Dwarven cooperative spirit never dies. -----------------------------------------------------|
## History

|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Better Together.|

|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Increased Better Together effect range from 10 blocks to 20.|
|Jul 8, 2025|0.23.1|Changed Better Together to account for players in the same zone.|

# Dark Monolith
Aliases: Monolith
Categories: Dwarven Mines, Mining, Blocks, Special items
[Dark Monolith]
The Dark Monolith is a Dragon Egg found in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It spawns randomly on 1 of 14 different locations and when mined or right-clicked, gives players a random amount of ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] or [[Coins]]. After it's mined, it will randomly teleport itself to another one of its locations within the [[Dwarven Mines]].
There is up to a 30s delay before another Dark Monolith can spawn. The first time a Dark Monolith is found, the player will receive the [[Achievements|"Hidden Secrets" Achievement]].
## Drop Chances
|Drop|Weight|Chance|
|-|-|-|
|100 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]|100|38.02% (1 in 2.63)|
|50,000 [[Coins]]|80|30.42% (1 in 3.29)|
|2,500 [[Coins]] and 1000 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]|50|19.01% (1 in 5.26)|
|3000 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]|30|11.41% (1 in 8.77)|
|[[Rock the Fish]]|3|1.14% (1 in 87.67)|

## Notes
- The Dark Monolith on a wooden platform being held up by a crane has the lowest chance to spawn.
- The Dark Monolith at the staircase in the Hanging Court has the highest chance to spawn.
- The Dark Monolith does not spawn at the exact coordinates listed and can be ±1 off in the X and/or Z directions.

# Raffle Box
Categories: Dwarven Mines
The Raffle Box
The Raffle Box is an object that appears during the [[Raffle|Raffle event]].
## Uses
[[Raffle Ticket|Raffle Tickets]] spawn as dropped items around the target location. Players try to collect as many tickets as possible and deposit them into the box.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Raffle Box.|

# Dragon Fragment
Aliases: Dragon Fragments, Holy Dragon Fragment, Old Dragon Fragment, Primal Dragon Fragment, Protector Dragon Fragment, Strong Dragon Fragment, Superior Dragon Fragment, Unstable Dragon Fragment, Wise Dragon Fragment, Young Dragon Fragment
Categories: Empty crafting usage, Crafting ingredients, Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Templates with deprecated argument names, Mob Drops
[Dragon Fragment]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]]); Medium Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]]); Large Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PROTECTOR_FRAGMENT; OLD_FRAGMENT; UNSTABLE_FRAGMENT; WISE_FRAGMENT; YOUNG_FRAGMENT; STRONG_FRAGMENT; SUPERIOR_FRAGMENT; HOLY_FRAGMENT
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC
Dragon Fragments are [[Rarity|EPIC]] items used to craft [[Dragon Armor|Dragon Armors]].
## Obtaining
### [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]]
Dragon Fragments are obtained by killing the [[Ender Dragon|Dragons]] at [[The End]]. The amount of Fragments the player receives is determined by the amount of [[Ender Dragon|Dragon Weight]] the player has.
### [[Lost Adventurer|Lost Adventurers]]
Young, Unstable, Superior, and Holy Fragments can also be obtained by killing [[Lost Adventurer|Lost Adventurers]] equipped with the respective [[Dragon Armor]] in [[Dungeons]]. This is the only way to obtain Holy Dragon Fragments. 1-3 Dragon Fragments are dropped by Lost Adventurers wearing Young, Unstable, or Holy Dragon Armor, and 1-2 Dragon Fragments are dropped by Lost Adventurers wearing Superior Dragon Armor.

Bane of Arthropods is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that increases damage dealt towards [[Arthropod]] mobs. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].
## Obtaining
The Bane of Arthropods [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Bane of Arthropods I|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Bane of Arthropods I|Purchased from [[Librarian]] for 20 [[Coins]]|
|Bane of Arthropods II–V|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Bane of Arthropods VI|Tier III [[Tarantula Broodfather]] drop 0.21% (1 in 485 kills) Tier IV [[Tarantula Broodfather]] drop 0.42% (1 in 240.54 kills) Tier V [[Tarantula Broodfather]] drop 0.35% (1 in 283.06 kills)|
|Bane of Arthropods VII; [[Ensnared Snail]] Endcap Upgrade ([[Endcaps]])|Tier V [[Tarantula Broodfather]] drop 0.042% (1 in 2,358.83 kills)|
|(same as above)|Ultra-rare drop from Supreme and above [[Experiments]]; (0.027% (1 in 3,700) chance from Metaphysical [[Experiments]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Bane of Arthropods) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Bane of Arthropods V.
### Bazaar
The Bane of Arthropods [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Bane of Arthropods|VI|
|[[Ensnared Snail]]|[[Ensnared Snail]]|
## Ability
Bane of Arthropods increases damage against [[Arthropod]] mobs.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|I|+5%|
|II|+10%|
|III|+15%|
|IV|+20%|
|V|+30%|
|VI|+40%|
|VII|+50%|
## Applying
Bane of Arthropods can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It does not require an [[Enchanting]] level to apply. Unlocking [[Spider Eye|Spider Eye V (5)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Bane of Arthropods.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|10 [[Experience]]|
|II|15 [[Experience]]|
|III|20 [[Experience]]|
|IV|25 [[Experience]]|
|V|30 [[Experience]]|
|VI|100 [[Experience]]|
|VII|200 [[Experience]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Bane of Arthropods.|
|Jun 26, 2019|0.4|Fixed Bane of Arthropods not applicable to [[Swords]] on an [[Anvil]].|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Changed Bane of Arthropods I crafting recipe to Bane of Arthropods IV and made it 8 times more expensive.|
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Added Bane of Arthropods VI as a drop from Tarantula Broodfathers ([[Tarantula Broodfather]]).|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Added Bane of Arthropods VII as a drop from [[Experiments]].|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Bane of Arthropods IV - Bane of Arthropods VI can now be dropped from [[Experiments]].|
|Feb 2, 2022|Changed scaling from 8/16/24/32/40/48/56% to 10/20/30/40/60/80/100% bonus damage.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Spider Eye V (5) ([[Spider Eye]]) now grants a -25% experience discount on Bane of Arthropods, instead of enchantment book recipe.|
|Jan 21, 2025|0.21.1|Removed Bane of Arthropods IV - Bane of Arthropods VI from [[Experiments]].|
|Jul 22, 2025|0.23.2|Replaced Bane of Arthropods VII with [[Ensnared Snail]].|
|Jul 22, 2025|0.23.2|Bane of Arthropods now works on all [[Arthropod]] mobs.|
|Sep 2, 2025|0.23.4|Fixed Bane of Arthropods VII still being obtainable from [[Experiments]].|
|Oct 15, 2025|0.23.5|Changed scaling from 10/20/30/40/60/80/100% to 5/10/15/20/30/40/50% bonus damage.|
|May 6, 2026|0.24.5|Added [[Enchanted Book]] (Bane of Arthropods VII) to the /legacytrades.|

[[Enchanted Book]] (Bobbin' Time) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Bobbin' Time V.

|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Bobbin' Time|III|
|Bobbin' Time|IV|
|Bobbin' Time|V|
## Ability
Increases some of your player stats by +0.6%~1% per fishing bobber within 30 blocks, up to 5 bobbers.
|Tier|Stat|Additive (Additive buffs are added together with each other, and then the sum is multiplied with the flat amount.) Bonus|
|-|-|-|
|Ⅲ|[[Fishing Speed]]|+0.6%|
|Ⅲ|[[Sea Creature Chance]]|+0.6%|
|Ⅲ|[[Double Hook Chance]]|+0.6%|
|Ⅲ|[[Trophy Chance]]|+0.6%|
|Ⅲ|[[Treasure Chance]]|+0.6%|
|Ⅲ|[[Magic Find]]|+0.6%|
|Ⅳ|[[Fishing Speed]]|+0.8%|
|Ⅳ|[[Sea Creature Chance]]|+0.8%|
|Ⅳ|[[Double Hook Chance]]|+0.8%|
|Ⅳ|[[Trophy Chance]]|+0.8%|
|Ⅳ|[[Treasure Chance]]|+0.8%|
|Ⅳ|[[Magic Find]]|+0.8%|
|Ⅴ|[[Fishing Speed]]|+1%|
|Ⅴ|[[Sea Creature Chance]]|+1%|
|Ⅴ|[[Double Hook Chance]]|+1%|
|Ⅴ|[[Trophy Chance]]|+1%|
|Ⅴ|[[Treasure Chance]]|+1%|
|Ⅴ|[[Magic Find]]|+1%|
## Applying
The Bobbin' Time [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to any [[Armor]] piece. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXIV (24)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅲ|100 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|120 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|140 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- This Enchant is a reference to the meme Morbin Time which was popular around the time of the release of this enchantment.
- The Bobbin' Time III book has the most unique raw materials required to craft, at 46. This is followed by [[Flaming Flay]] at 29 and [[Will-o'-wisp]] at 21.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:

Chimera is an Ultimate [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] that copies a [[Pets|Pet]]'s stats. It does not copy stats granted by pet abilities, but it does copy stats added to the pet's base stats by [[Pet Items]]. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXXI (31)]].

[[Enchanted Book]] (Chimera) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Chimera V.

|-|-|
|Ⅰ|+20%|
|Ⅱ|+40%|
|Ⅲ|+60%|
|Ⅳ|+80%|
|Ⅴ|+100%|
## Applying
The Chimera [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be applied to any [[Weapons|Weapon]]. This includes [[Swords]], [[Longswords]] and [[Gemstone Gauntlet|Gauntlets]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXXI (31)]] to apply.

## Trivia
- The name of the [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]] is a reference to the creature from Greek mythology, which had a lion's head, goat's body and serpent's tail.

Cleave is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that deals up to 5-30% of the player's [[Damage]] dealt to other monsters within 3.5-6 blocks of the target. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting IV (4)]].

Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Moonflower VII (7) ([[Moonflower]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: V
Applied To: [[Farming Tools]]
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XXXII (32) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_ULTIMATE_CROP_FEVER
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Crop Fever I When breaking crops, there is a 0.001% chance to trigger Crop Fever for 60s which grants:; - Additional crop rolls - +15 Overbloom - +100 Farming Fortune; You can only have 1 Ultimate Enchantment on an item!; Applicable on: Farming Tool Apply Cost: 45 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 32 to apply.; COMMON
Crop Fever is an Ultimate [[Ultimate Enchantments|Enchantment]]. It can be applied to [[Farming Tools]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXXII (32)]].
## Obtaining
The Crop Fever [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Book]] (Crop Fever I)|Moonflower VII (7) ([[Moonflower]])|1x [[Jukebox]] 16x [[Compacted Moonflower]]|Recipe: [[Compacted Moonflower]] x2 ×8 + [[Jukebox]] → Enchanted Book|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Crop Fever) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Crop Fever V.
### Bazaar
The Crop Fever [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Crop Fever|I|
|Crop Fever|II|
|Crop Fever|III|
|Crop Fever|IV|
|Crop Fever|V|
## Ability
While breaking crops, the player has a 0.001% chance per level to trigger a Crop Fever, which lasts for 60 seconds. During a Crop Fever, the player gains +100 [[Farming Fortune]], 15 [[Overbloom]], and each crop broken has a chance to grant additional crops.
### Drop Types
|Drop Rarity|Weight|Chance|
|-|-|-|
|None|20,000||
|UNCOMMON|90|0.4469% (1 in 223.76 crops)|
|RARE|40|0.1986% (1 in 503.45 crops)|
|CRAZY RARE|7|0.03476% (1 in 2,876.86 crops)|
|PRAY TO RNGESUS|1|0.004966% (1 in 20,138 crops)|
### Drop Amounts
|Crop|Drop Rarity UNCOMMON|Drop Rarity RARE|Drop Rarity CRAZY RARE|Drop Rarity PRAY TO RNGESUS|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wheat]]|6 [[Enchanted Wheat]]|10 [[Enchanted Wheat]]|120 (1 stack plus 56) [[Enchanted Wheat]]|12 [[Enchanted Hay Bale]]|
|[[Carrot]]|18 [[Enchanted Carrot]]|42 [[Enchanted Carrot]]|4 [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]]|12 [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]]|
|[[Potato]]|18 [[Enchanted Potato]]|32 [[Enchanted Potato]]|2 [[Enchanted Baked Potato]]|6 [[Enchanted Baked Potato]]|
|[[Pumpkin]]|6 [[Enchanted Pumpkin]]|12 [[Enchanted Pumpkin]]|110 (1 stack plus 46) [[Enchanted Pumpkin]]|4 [[Polished Pumpkin]]|
|[[Sugar Cane]]|12 [[Enchanted Sugar]]|24 [[Enchanted Sugar]]|1 [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]]|6 [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]]|
|[[Melon Slice]]|24 [[Enchanted Melon Slice]]|48 [[Enchanted Melon Slice]]|4 [[Enchanted Melon]]|12 [[Enchanted Melon]]|
|[[Cactus]]|6 [[Enchanted Cactus Green]]|18 [[Enchanted Cactus Green]]|1 [[Enchanted Cactus]]|6 [[Enchanted Cactus]]|
|[[Cocoa Beans]]|6 [[Enchanted Cocoa Beans]]|12 [[Enchanted Cocoa Beans]]|120 [[Enchanted Cocoa Beans]]|4 [[Enchanted Cookie]]|
|Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|6 [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]]|12 [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]]|120 [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]]|16 [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom Block]]|
|Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|6 [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]]|12 [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]]|120 [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]]|16 [[Enchanted Red Mushroom Block]]|
|[[Nether Wart]]|18 [[Enchanted Nether Wart]]|36 [[Enchanted Nether Wart]]|2 [[Mutant Nether Wart]]|6 [[Mutant Nether Wart]]|
|[[Sunflower]]|12 [[Enchanted Sunflower]]|24 [[Enchanted Sunflower]]|2 [[Compacted Sunflower]]|6 [[Compacted Sunflower]]|
|[[Moonflower]]|12 [[Enchanted Moonflower]]|24 [[Enchanted Moonflower]]|2 [[Compacted Moonflower]]|6 [[Compacted Moonflower]]|
|[[Wild Rose]]|12 [[Enchanted Wild Rose]]|24 [[Enchanted Wild Rose]]|2 [[Compacted Wild Rose]]|6 [[Compacted Wild Rose]]|
When a Crop Fever is activated, the following message is displayed:
WOAH! You caught a case of the CROP FEVER for 60 seconds!
Once it expires, the following message is displayed:
GONE! Your CROP FEVER has been cured!
## Applying
Crop Fever can be applied to [[Farming Tools]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXXII (32)]] to apply.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|45 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅱ|91 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅲ|136 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅳ|179 [[Experience]]|
|Ⅴ|223 [[Experience]]|
## Bugs
- Crop Fever procs twice on [[Sugar Cane]] and [[Cactus]] due to them being two block tall crops.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; is this still true? it might've been fixed. If it's intended this category should be renamed from 'Bugs' to 'Notes'; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
## Trivia
- While under the effect of a Crop Fever, the player's [[Health]], [[Defense]], and [[Mana]] displays alternate between rainbow colors, and a unique soundtrack plays if the player has Hypixel music enabled.
  - Like many other Hypixel soundtracks, it was made by ShinkoNet.
- Crop Fever's ability works similarly to how [[Pumpkin Dicer]] and [[Melon Dicer]]'s abilities worked before the update that released it.
- Upon release, Crop Fever was dropping 120 [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] for the PRAY TO RNGESUS drop for [[Wheat]], which is worth 3,072,000 (48,000 stacks) [[Wheat]], or 18,432,000 [[Coins]] to [[Shops|NPC]]. This was later patched and became 12 [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] instead.
  - The mistake likely came from the fact that the CRAZY RARE drop is 120 [[Enchanted Wheat]].
- Crop Fever can be activated from breaking [[Mutations]] in the [[Greenhouse]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Crop Fever.|
|Mar 31, 2026|Crop Fever can now be applied to [[Farming Tools]], instead of [[Axes]] and [[Hoes]].|
|Apr 21, 2026|Changed Rare Crop ([[Rare Crops]]) chance to [[Overbloom]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Crop Fever.|

|Feb 4, 2026|Increased PRAY TO RNGESUS drop for [[Nether Wart]] from 2 [[Mutant Nether Wart]] to 6 [[Mutant Nether Wart]].|

|II|+10%|
|III|+15%|
|IV|+20%|

|VI|+40%|
## Applying
Cubism can be applied to [[Swords]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XXV (25)]] to apply. Unlocking [[Pumpkin|Pumpkin IV (4)]] grants an -25% [[Experience]] discount on Cubism.

|Drain V|Ultra-rare drop from Supreme and above [[Experiments]]; (0.027% (1 in 3,700) chance from Metaphysical [[Experiments]])|

|I|0.5|
|II|1|
|III|1.5|
|IV|2|
|V|2.5|
|VI (Blood-Soaked ([[Reforging]]))|3|

## Applying

|IV|50 [[Experience]]|
|V|200 [[Experience]]|

|Ⅰ|1.1x|
|Ⅱ|+8%|
|Ⅱ|1.2x|
|Ⅲ|+12%|
|Ⅲ|1.3x|
|Ⅳ|+16%|

|Ⅴ|+20%|
|Ⅴ|1.5x|

|III|70 [[Mining Speed]]|
|IV|90 [[Mining Speed]]|
|V|110 [[Mining Speed]]|
|VI|130 [[Mining Speed]]|
|VII|150 [[Mining Speed]]|
|VIII|170 [[Mining Speed]]|
|IX|190 [[Mining Speed]]|
|X|210 [[Mining Speed]]|
For how Efficiency works in other cases, see Efficiency (Minecraft Wiki).
## Applying
Efficiency can be applied to [[Tools]]. Unlocking [[Redstone Dust|Redstone Dust III (3)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Efficiency.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|10 [[Experience]]|
|II|15 [[Experience]]|
|III|20 [[Experience]]|
|IV|25 [[Experience]]|
|V|30 [[Experience]]|
## Trivia
- [[Stonk]] comes with Efficiency VI enchantment, but [[Silex]] cannot be applied to it.
- [[Lazy Miner]]'s [[Iron Pickaxe]] comes with Efficiency I enchantment.
- Before the addition of [[Dungeonbreaker]], players used [[Diamond Pickaxe]] with Efficiency X to glitch through walls for faster runs and [[Dungeon Secrets|Secret]] gathering.
  - It was called Stonking, derivative of the [[Stonk]] item, since it was used before players figured out the best way to do it.
  - [[Dungeonbreaker]] also has Efficiency X while in [[Dungeons]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Efficiency I–V.|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Changed Efficiency I crafting recipe to Efficiency IV and made it 8 times more expensive.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Efficiency IV–V can now be dropped from [[Experiments]].|
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Efficiency now grants [[Mining Speed]] on some islands.|
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added [[Silex]] crafing recipe, which allows to get Efficiency X.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Redstone Dust III (3) ([[Redstone Dust]]) now grants a -25% experience discount on Efficiency, instead of enchantment book recipe.|
|Aug 6, 2024|0.20.5|Fixed Efficiency not showing [[Silex]] in the Enchantment Guide ([[Enchantment Nerd]]).|
|Jan 21, 2025|0.21.1|Efficiency IV–V can no longer be dropped from [[Experiments]].|

## Search
e [[Enchantments/Search|v]]
Search script not loaded yet.
## Overview
Enchanting improves gear ([[Armor]], [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]], [[Farming Tools]], [[Shears]], [[Swords]], [[Gauntlet]], [[Axes]], [[Fishing Rods]], [[Shovels]] and [[Bows]]) to make them stronger than they already are and improve them with buffs and stat boosts.
Enchanting items also levels up the [[Enchanting]] [[Skills|Skill]], and certain enchantments cannot be used by the player if they are not at a certain enchanting skill level.
Books cannot be used in an enchantment table. [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]] are free to combine and do not add anvil uses. Additionally, some enchantments with max level V or III have a higher level book or [[Endcaps|Endcap Upgrade]] unobtainable via the enchantment table.

## Enchantment Table
The Enchantment Table can apply up to Level V enchantments to items. Enchanting in SkyBlock has a set [[Experience]] cost for every enchant and every level. Most enchants in the game cost 30-50 levels for the max tier obtainable through a enchantment table, but some higher grade enchants, such as [[First Strike|First Strike IV]], cost up to 75 levels.
Unlike vanilla Minecraft, even if an item is already enchanted, it can still be used in the Enchantment Table. The Enchantment Table lets the user choose the Enchantments to add to or remove from the item in the slot on the left at will. All the valid enchantments will be listed on the side, and each enchantment (and every tier of it) will cost various amounts of [[Experience|Experience Levels]].
When an item is placed within the Enchantment Table, a list of all applicable enchantments will be shown. Clicking on a desired enchantment shows all possible levels of the enchantment, along with their XP costs. Clicking on the desired level will then apply the enchantment, provided the player has enough XP.
Applying conflicting enchantments within the table (for example, [[Drain]] to a weapon with [[Life Steal]]) will cause the old enchantment to be replaced with the new one.
Additionally, enchantments can be removed through the [[Enchantment Table]] (even the ones that unobtainable in the table) or [[The Hex]] by clicking on the same enchantment and level. They cost the same amount of XP to remove and also grant [[Enchanting]] XP.
Books can not be enchanted in a enchantment table directly, and can only be obtained by experiments or other sources.
Certain enchantments have higher tiers which are unavailable within the Enchantment Table; they must be obtained by other means, often through the [[Experimentation Table]].
## High-Level Enchanted Books
Unlike normal enchantments, the top level of many enchants - often Level VI - cannot be found on the [[Enchantment Table]], and cannot be made by combining two level V books. They are instead obtained by special means, such as the [[Experimentation Table]], the [[Dark Auction]], or [[Tomioka]]. In cases where enchantments don't normally go to level V, the top-level enchant maybe be smaller than VI (ex: Life Steal IV, which normally goes only up to III).
## Endcap Upgrades
[[Endcaps|Endcap Upgrades]] are items that upgrade an Enchantment to a higher tier, requiring its respective Enchantment's immediately preceding tier to be applied. They can be combined with items in an [[Anvil]].
Most Endcap Upgrades can also be obtained from the [[Experimentation Table]], with the exception of those obtained from crafting.
## Ultimate Enchantments
Ultimate Enchantments are more powerful Enchantments which can be added to items in an anvil. Only 1 Ultimate Enchantment can be applied to an item. If another one is applied, the previous one will be overwritten.
## Bazaar
The Books for Enchantments that cannot be obtained through an [[Enchantment Table]] can be bought and sold on the [[Bazaar]].
- [[Enchanted Book/Bazaar (Normal)|Tab 1]]
## Trivia
- Certain items with otherwise unobtainable or illegal enchantments exist. They were created by 12[[Ranks|ADMIN]]s.
  - [[Protection|Protection L]] - Grants +150 [[Defense]] - Worn by 12 Plancke on an [[Anubis]] and an [[Armor of the Stars]] set
  - [[Protection|Protection X]] - Grants +30 [[Defense]] - Worn by 12 Plancke on a [[Titan's Armor|Titan's Chestplate]], [[Titan's Armor|Titan's Leggings]], [[Titan's Armor|Titan's Boots]], a [[Boss (helmet)|Boss]], and an [[Armor of the Stars]] set
  - [[True Protection|True Protection X]] - Grants +50 [[True Defense]] - Worn by 12 Plancke on [[Titan's Armor|Titan's Chestplate]]
  - [[Sugar Rush|Sugar Rush X]] - Grants +20 [[Speed]] - Worn by 12 Plancke on [[Titan's Armor|Titan's Boots]] and [[Armor of the Stars|Boots of the Stars]]
  - [[Growth|Growth L]] - Grants +750 [[Health]] - Worn by 12 AgentKid on a [[Boss (helmet)|Boss]] and [[Armor of the Stars]]
  - [[Growth|Growth M]] - Grants +15000 [[Health]] - Worn by 12 Plancke on an [[Anubis]]
  - Growth TOO_HIGH_NUMBER - Grants +75000 [[Health]] - Worn by 12 Plancke on a [[Titan's Armor|Titan's Chestplate]], [[Titan's Armor|Titan's Leggings]], [[Titan's Armor|Titan's Boots]], a [[Boss (helmet)|Boss]], and an [[Armor of the Stars]] set
    - This level of Growth is Equivalent to Growth V̅ (5,000).
  - [[Sharpness|Sharpness XX]] - Increases [[Damage|melee damage]] by 200% - Used by 12 Plancke on a [[Pigman Sword]]
  - (Various Enchants) L - Used by 12 Minikloon on an [[Aspect of the Dragons]]
  - [[First Strike|First Strike V]], [[Triple-Strike|Triple-Strike V]], [[Life Steal|Life Steal V]] with [[Drain|Drain IV]] - Used by 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] 56ms on a [[Valkyrie]] given by 12Jayavarmen, which has since been thrown into the void.
  - [[Vicious|Vicious VI]] - On a [[Valkyrie]] owned by 5DragonBoii, given by an 12[[Ranks|ADMIN]] as a replacement, most likely with the unobtainable enchant by mistake.
  - Sugar Rush TOO_HIGH_NUMBER - Grants +200 [[Speed]] - Worn by 12 TorWolf on [[Armor of the Stars|Boots of the Stars]]
    - This level of Sugar Rush is Equivalent to Sugar Rush C (100).
  - [[Power|Power L]] - Increases [[Damage|bow damage]] by 400% -Used by 12 TorWolf on a [[Raygun]]
  - [[Sharpness|Sharpness XXV]] - Increases [[Damage|melee damage]] by 125% - Used by 12 TorWolf on a [[Sword of the Stars|Sword of the Stars 3000]]
  - [[Chimera|Chimera XXV]] - Used by 12 TorWolf on a [[Rogue Sword]]
  - [[Sharpness|Sharpness L]] - Increases [[Damage|melee damage]] by 250% - Used by 12 TorWolf on a [[Sword of the Stars|Sword of the Stars 9000]]
  - Telekinesis O - Normal [[Telekinesis (Enchantment)|Telekinesis]] effects - Seen in 12 TorWolf's inventory
  - [[Overload|Overload X]] - Increases [[Crit Chance]] by 10% and [[Crit Damage]] by 10%. Having a Critical chance above 100% grants a chance to perform a Mega Critical Hit dealing 100%. - Seen in 12 TorWolf's inventory
- The regular vanilla enchantment, Infinity, is one of the few vanilla enchantments not in SkyBlock. It is replaced in the form [[Infinite Quiver]]. It used to be possible to get [[Infinite Quiver|Infinite Quiver X]] from dungeons, which would effectively function the same as Infinity would, although this was changed on the 7th of September 2021.
- The enchantment GUI used to work similarly to vanilla, where 3 different options for enchanting were shown and the enchantments received were unknown. This was changed in 0.10.
  - This is the same for vanilla enchantments added after 1.8.9 due to server compatibility issues.
- There is a very common misconception with [[Giant Killer]] that states that the maximum damage is 25% and that higher levels affect the rate at which Giant Killer increases damage.
  - The 25% refers to the enemy's HP, not the damage multiplier cap. [[Giant Killer]] takes the enemy's HP and the player's HP. Then, if the enemy's HP is 25% or higher (1.25x the player's HP), it'll use 25% to calculate the damage increase, which is then calculated as follows:
    - Damage multiplier = 1 + 0.25 * [[Giant Killer]] level * 0.1, when enemy HP >= 1.25 * player HP and 0.25 is the HP cap. Thus, the maximum damage increase from [[Giant Killer]] is 2.5%/5%/7.5%/10%/12.5%/15%/17.5% from [[Giant Killer|Giant Killer I]] to [[Giant Killer|Giant Killer VII]] respectively.
- The [[Replenish]] enchantment used to be partly bugged when using a [[Compactor (disambiguation)|Personal Compactor]] and harvesting crops. Sometimes, it would compact all crops into the enchanted form, leaving no crop for [[Replenish]] to replant. When this happened, it left an empty patch rather than replanting the crop. This could be remedied with an [[Agronomy Sack]], which will collect the crop before it is compacted, and which the [[Replenish]] enchant can draw from.
  - This was fixed in the 0.19.8 update.
- The recipes of [[Sharpness|Sharpness IV]] and [[Protection|Protection IV]] were references to the recipe of Book of Sharpening and Book of Protection in another Hypixel minigame, UHC Champions, which uses the same recipes.
- [[Dragon Tracer]] used to be known as Aiming. It had the same effect except it worked on almost every mob type.
  - This enchantment seems to also work on other bosses.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- Logs and partially grown crops both increase the counter for the [[Cultivating]] enchantment, but only on [[Axes]].
- The [[Blessing (Enchantment)|Blessing]] enchantment can trigger while fishing a fairy soul, but you only get one soul, however you get the fortunate achievement.
- The [[Cultivating]] enchantment's counter also currently counts Seeds collected from Wheat as "crops" when placed on a Hoe. This was discovered when the enchantment was placed on a newly crafted Euclid's Hoe and the counters began diverging.
- [[Efficiency|Efficiency X]] used to be used on a [[Pickaxes|Vanilla Pickaxe]] to speed up [[Dungeon Secrets|Secret]] Finding, but it has been made obsolete because permanent Fatigue is applied to all players in dungeons. However, a [[Dungeonbreaker]] can be used for the same effect.
- [[Telekinesis (Enchantment)|Telekinesis]] Enchanted Books became glitched Enchanted Books with no Enchantments due to the replacement of [[Telekinesis (Enchantment)|Telekinesis]] with [[Auto-pickup]].
- When used below [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level]] 6, the [[Scavenger]] enchantment will drop the coins as actual items in the world.
- An update from December 16, 2021 was said to have fixed [[Chance]], [[Looting]], and [[Luck]], but as a result of this change, [[Looting]] doesn't work on [[Slayer|Slayers]].
- [[Aqua Affinity]], [[Delicate]], [[Great Spook (Enchantment)|Great Spook]], [[Piercing]], [[Replenish]], [[Silk Touch]], [[Smelting Touch]], [[Stealth]], and [[True Protection]] are the only enchantments to only have one tier.
- [[Habanero Tactics]], [[Big Brain]], [[Cayenne]], [[Counter-Strike]], [[Delicate]], [[Quantum]], [[Small Brain]], [[Tabasco]], [[Transylvanian]], and [[Vicious]] are the only enchantments without tier 1.

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Enchantments.|
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|The enchantment table works similarly to the vanilla enchantment table. When a non-enchanted item is inserted, 3 different enchanting options will appear, each option costing different amounts of XP. The 3rd option always costs the maximum amount of XP from the enchantment table based on the number of bookshelves placed around it. Each option shows 1 enchantment which is guaranteed to be applied, but it is possible for more to be applied.|
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|The list of the lowest possible amount of XP required to be spent to obtain certain enchantments can be found here ([[Enchantment Table/Levels (Pre-0.10)]]).|
|Jun 20, 2019|0.3|Tweaked the [[Lure]] enchantment to affect the maximum time it takes to catch an item.|
|Jun 20, 2019|0.3|Improved [[Smelting Touch]] to work on [[Sand]] ([[Glass]]), Wood ([[Wood (disambiguation)]]) ([[Charcoal]]), [[Cactus]] ([[Cactus Green]]), [[Cobblestone]] ([[Stone]]), [[Netherrack]] ([[Nether Brick]]), and [[Clay]] ([[Brick]]).|
|Jun 20, 2019|0.3|Halved the selling price of [[Silk Touch]] and [[Growth]].|
|Jun 20, 2019|0.3|-25 [[Health]] on Growth V ([[Growth]]) only (from +100 to +75).|
|Jun 26, 2019|0.4|-7% [[Thorns]] enchantment damage (from 10% to 3%).|
|Jun 26, 2019|0.4|Changed [[Giant Killer]] to now add +0.5% extra damage for each extra %[[Health]] the enemy has above your own.|
|Jun 29, 2019|+1 [[Defense]] per level on [[Protection]] (from 2 to 3).|

|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Revamped Enchanting.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|The enchantment table can now add and remove enchantments on any item, including enchanted ones, for XP cost.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Added an enchanting guide which shows details of all enchantments. It can be accessed in the enchantment table menu or with /e.g.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Books can no longer be enchanted directly.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Enchanted Books ([[Enchanted Book]]) no longer use anvil uses and are now completely free to combine. The cost of applying an enchanted book is the combined cost of the XP orbs of all of the enchantments on the book but with a discount of 25%. Each book also shows the number of levels required to apply it in its lore.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Leveling up the enchanting skill now gives access to specific enchantments and also grants +0.5% [[Ability Damage]] per level.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Most enchantments now require a certain enchanting skill level to apply and use.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|There is a 2-week grace period where players can use any enchantments regardless of their enchanting skill requirement in order to give players time to level up their enchanting skill. After the grace period, any enchantments on gear that a player does not have the required skill level for will be greyed out and will not work (the gear itself and all other enchantments will function normally).|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Capped the enchanting XP one can gain from the enchantment table and anvil to 50k XP per day.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Experiments can be conducted in the Experimentation Table which is unlocked at enchanting level 10 and can be placed on private islands. At this table, one can conduct daily experiments which are fun minigames and grant loads of enchanting skill XP ("like 1 million") and special rewards such as Experience Bottles and high-level enchantment books.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Chance I–V ([[Chance]]), Drain I–V ([[Drain]]), Triple-Strike I–V ([[Triple-Strike]]), Thunderbolt I–VI ([[Thunderbolt]]), Respite I–V ([[Respite]]), Prosecute I–VI ([[Prosecute]]), Titan Killer I–VII ([[Titan Killer]]).|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Blast Protection VI–VII ([[Blast Protection]]), Fire Protection VI–VII ([[Fire Protection]]), Looting V ([[Looting]]), Luck VII ([[Luck]]), Cubism VI ([[Cubism]]), Cleave VI ([[Cleave]]), Life Steal V ([[Life Steal]]), Snipe IV ([[Snipe]]), Critical VII ([[Critical]]), First Strike V ([[First Strike]]), Ender Slayer VII ([[Ender Slayer]]), Execute VI ([[Execute]]), Venomous VI ([[Venomous]]).|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Added new commands to the cookie buff: /et or /etable to access an enchantment table, and /av or /anvil to access an anvil.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Balanced XP level cost of all enchantments.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Buffed [[Fire Aspect]] so it now deals up to 100% of the weapon damage as fire damage per second.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Changed the visual effect of [[Fire Aspect]], it no longer sets enemies on fire on hit, but displays flame particles and shows the damage in an orange damage tag.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Buffed Thunderlord from 10% to 25% per level.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Nerfed Rend to only work with 5 arrows at a time.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Buffed [[Fire Protection]] from +4 defense per level to +60 defense per level and +2 true defense against fire or lava. Now works with the Campfire Trial.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Buffed [[Blast Protection]] from +4 defense per level to +20 defense per level.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Buffed [[Projectile Protection]] from +3 defense per level to +7 defense per level.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|[[Giant Killer]] bonus damage cap now scales with its level by 5% per level. Previously, all levels had a fixed damage bonus cap of 25%.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Reduced required level for many powerful enchantments.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Daily Enchanting XP cap from non-experiment sources raised to 500k (from 50k).|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Experiments can now be replayed up to 3 times per day, for a total of 4 per day, at the cost of XP and bits.|

|Mar 30, 2021|0.11.3|Changed [[Drain]] to heal (0.1% + 0.1% per level) per 100 Crit Damage up to 1000, of max health on hit, instead of (0.1% + 0.1%) of damage dealt per hit.|
|Mar 30, 2021|0.11.3|Ferocity hits no longer trigger [[Life Steal]] or [[Drain]].|

# Ender Slayer
Categories: Enchantments
[Ender Slayer]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: VII
Applied To: [[Swords]], Gauntlets ([[Gemstone Gauntlet]]), [[Longswords]]
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XI (11) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_ENDER_SLAYER
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Ender Slayer I Increases damage dealt to  Ender mobs by 5%.; Applicable on: Melee Weapon Apply Cost: 10 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 11 to apply.; COMMON
Ender Slayer is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that increases damage dealt towards [[Ender]] mobs. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting XI (11)]].
## Obtaining
The Ender Slayer [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Ender Slayer I–V|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Ender Slayer VI|Purchased from [[Pearl Dealer]] for 1,500,000 [[Coins]]|
|Ender Slayer VII; [[End Stone Idol]] Endcap Upgrade ([[Endcaps]])|Tier IV [[Voidgloom Seraph]] drop 0.014% (1 in 7,084.5 kills)|
|(same as above)|Ultra-rare drop from Supreme and above [[Experiments]]; (0.027% (1 in 3,700) chance from Metaphysical [[Experiments]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Ender Slayer) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Ender Slayer V.
### Bazaar
The Ender Slayer [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Ender Slayer|VI|
|[[End Stone Idol]]|[[End Stone Idol]]|
## Ability
Ender Slayer increases damage against [[Ender]] mobs.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|I|+5%|
|II|+10%|
|III|+15%|
|IV|+20%|
|V|+30%|
|VI|+40%|
|VII|+50%|
## Applying
Ender Slayer can be applied to [[Swords]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XI (11)]] to apply. Unlocking [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearl III (3)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Ender Slayer.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|10 [[Experience]]|
|II|20 [[Experience]]|
|III|25 [[Experience]]|
|IV|30 [[Experience]]|
|V|40 [[Experience]]|
|VI|75 [[Experience]]|
|VII|179 [[Experience]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Ender Slayer.|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Changed Ender Slayer I crafting recipe to Ender Slayer IV and made it 8 times more expensive.|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Ender Slayer VI as a purchase from the [[Pearl Dealer]].|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Ender Slayer IV - Ender Slayer V can now be dropped from [[Experiments]].|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Added Ender Slayer VII as a drop from [[Experiments]].|
|Feb 2, 2022|Changed scaling from 12/24/36/48/60/72/84% to 15/30/45/60/80/100/130% bonus damage.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Ender Pearl III (3) ([[Ender Pearl]]) now grants a -25% experience discount on Ender Slayer, instead of enchantment book recipe.|
|Mar 18, 2024|Ender Slayer now works on Endermites ([[Endermite]]).|
|Jan 21, 2025|0.21.1|Removed Ender Slayer IV - Ender Slayer VI from [[Experiments]].|
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Replaced Ender Slayer VII with [[End Stone Idol]].|
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Ender Slayer now works on all [[Ender]] mobs, instead of Ender Dragons, Endermen and Endermites.|
|Aug 14, 2025|Ender Slayer can now be used with other mob-specific enchantments.|
|Oct 15, 2025|0.23.5|Changed scaling from 15/30/45/60/80/100/130% to 5/10/15/20/30/40/50% bonus damage.|
|May 6, 2026|0.24.5|Added Ender Slayer VII to the /legacytrades.|

# Execute
Categories: Enchantments
[Execute]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: VI
Applied To: [[Swords]], Gauntlets ([[Gemstone Gauntlet]]), [[Longswords]]
Req. [[Enchanting]] Level: Enchanting XIV (14) ([[Enchanting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_EXECUTE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Execute I Increases damage dealt by 0.2% for each percent of health missing on your target.; Applicable on: Melee Weapon Apply Cost: 20 Exp Levels; Requires Enchanting 14 to apply.; COMMON
Execute is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that increases damage dealt for every percentage of health the target is missing. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]], starting at [[Enchanting|Enchanting XIV (14)]], and is mutually exclusive with [[Prosecute]].
## Obtaining
The Execute [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Execute I–V|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Execute VI|16.67% chance to appear in a [[Dark Auction]]|
|Execute VI|Ultra-rare drop from Supreme and above [[Experiments]]; (0.027% (1 in 3,700) chance from Metaphysical [[Experiments]])|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Execute) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Execute V.
### Bazaar
The Execute [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Execute|VI|
## Ability
Execute increases damage dealt for every percentage of health the target has.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|I|+0.2%|
|II|+0.4%|
|III|+0.6%|
|IV|+0.8%|
|V|+1%|
|VI|+1.25%|
## Applying
Execute can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], [[Gauntlet]]. It requires [[Enchanting|Enchanting XIV (14)]] to apply. Unlocking [[Diamond|Diamond II (2)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Execute.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|20 [[Experience]]|
|II|25 [[Experience]]|
|III|30 [[Experience]]|
|IV|40 [[Experience]]|
|V|50 [[Experience]]|
|VI|200 [[Experience]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Execute I–III.|
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Added Execute IV–V.|
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Nerfed from 1% [[Damage]] to 0.2% [[Damage]] per tier.|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Changed Execute I crafting recipe to Execute IV and made it 8 times more expensive.|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Execute IV - Execute V can now be dropped from [[Experiments]].|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Added Execute VI as a drop from [[Experiments]].|
|Feb 2, 2022|Execute VI buffed from 1.2% [[Damage]] to 1.25% [[Damage]].|
|Jun 21, 2023|Fixed weapon swap with Execute.|
|Jul 25, 2023|Execute now applies to magic damage.|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Diamond II (2) ([[Diamond]]) now grants a -25% [[Experience]] on Execute, instead of enchantment book recipe.|
|Jan 21, 2025|0.21.1|Removed Execute IV - Execute V from [[Experiments]].|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Execute VI is now obtainable from [[Dark Auction]].|

# Experience (Enchantment)
Aliases: Experience Enchantment
Categories: Enchantments
[Experience (Enchantment)]
Type: Enchantment ([[Enchantments]])
[Enchantment Requirements]
Minimum Level: I
Maximum Level: V
Applied To: [[Swords]], [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]], Gauntlets ([[Gemstone Gauntlet]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTMENT_EXPERIENCE
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Experience I Grants a 12.5% chance for mobs and ores to drop double experience.; Applicable on: Melee Weapon, Mining Tools Apply Cost: 15 Exp Levels; COMMON
Experience is an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] that grants a chance for [[Mob|Mobs]] and [[Ores]] to drop double [[Experience]]. It can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]], [[Pickaxes]], and [[Drills]].
## Obtaining
The Experience [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be obtained in various ways:
|Enchantment Tier|Enchantment Source|
|-|-|
|Experience I–III|[[Enchantment Table]]|
|Experience IV|Purchased from Joyful Viking ([[Melancholic Viking]]) for 400,000 [[Coins]].|
|Experience V|Purchased from [[Frozen Alex]] for 15,000 [[Ice Essence]].|
[[Enchanted Book]] (Experience) can be combined on an [[Anvil]] up to Experience III.
### Bazaar
The Experience [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] can be traded through the [[Bazaar]].
|Enchantment|Tier|
|-|-|
|Experience|IV|
|Experience|V|
## Ability
Experience grants a chance for mobs and ores to grant double Experience.
|Tier|Bonus|
|-|-|
|I|+12.5%|
|II|+25%|
|III|+37.5%|
|IV|+50%|
|V|+62.5%|
## Applying
Experience can be applied to melee [[Weapons]], [[Pickaxes]], and [[Drills]]. It does not require an [[Enchanting]] level to apply. Unlocking [[Lapis Lazuli|Lapis Lazuli III (3)]] grants a -25% [[Experience]] discount on Experience.
|Tier|Cost|
|-|-|
|I|15 [[Experience]]|
|II|30 [[Experience]]|
|III|45 [[Experience]]|
|IV|75 [[Experience]]|
|V|0 [[Experience]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Experience I–III.|
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Changed Experience I crafting recipe to Experience II and made it 2 times more expensive.|
|Nov 11, 2019|0.7.4|Added Experience IV as an item from Joyful Viking ([[Melancholic Viking]]).|
|Dec 4, 2020|0.10|Experience II–III can now be dropped from [[Experiments]].|
|Dec 1, 2022|0.17|Added Experience V as an item from [[Frozen Alex]].|
|Feb 6, 2024|0.19.11|Lapis Lazuli III (3) ([[Lapis Lazuli]]) now grants a -25% experience discount on Experience, instead of enchantment book recipe.|
|Jan 21, 2025|0.21.1|Experience II–III can no longer be dropped from [[Experiments]].|
